### [72] [A Controlled Benchmark of Quantum-Latent GAN Augmentation for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18970)
*Syed Mujtaba Haider,Silvia Figini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过严格控制对照基准实验，发现基于变分量子生成器的脑MRI数据增强方法在所有标注数据比例（5%-100%）下均未显著优于仅使用真实数据的训练，且量子生成器与参数相近的经典生成器在性能上无统计差异；量子生成增强的任何低数据优势本质上是正则化效应而非真实数据扩展，其合成样本存在分布外偏移和严重的模式坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于量子生成模型用于医学图像增强并提升精度的声称通常基于单次训练、未匹配经典与量子生成器的参数预算、且未刻画增益出现的数据区间，缺乏严格对照的基准验证。

Method: 将脑MRI图像编码到KL正则化潜空间，在潜空间中训练条件Wasserstein GAN（梯度惩罚），分别使用变分量子生成器和参数近乎相同的经典生成器（1648 vs 1632参数），生成样本经解码后用于增强预训练分类器；在标注数据比例5%-100%范围内，基于8个随机种子进行配对显著性检验（多重比较校正）以及集内多样性和潜分布分析。

Result: 在所有标注数据比例下，任何增强变体均未显著优于仅用真实数据训练；量子与经典生成器在统计上不可区分；低数据下的任何增益表现为正则化而非忠实数据扩展——合成样本在数据稀缺时呈现分布外偏移和严重的模式坍塌，且量子生成器的多样性不优于经典生成器。

Conclusion: 当前的量子生成增强方法在医学图像分类中并未带来实质性的数据扩展增益，其看似优势实为正则化效应；需要更严格的基准测试来评估量子生成模型在医学影像中的实际价值，作者发布了该协议作为评估基准。

Abstract: Medical image classification is often constrained by limited labeled data, motivating generative augmentation; recently, quantum generative models have been proposed for this purpose, frequently reporting accuracy gains. However, such claims are typically based on single training runs, do not match the parameter budgets of the quantum and classical generators, and do not characterize the data regime in which any benefit appears. We present a controlled benchmark that isolates the contribution of a quantum generator to brain-MRI augmentation. Images are encoded into a KL-regularized latent space in which a conditional Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty is trained using either a variational quantum generator or a classical generator of near-identical parameter count (1648 vs. 1632). Synthetic samples are decoded and used to augment a pretrained classifier across labeled data fractions from 5% to 100%, evaluated over eight random seeds with paired significance testing (with multiple-comparison correction) and with intraset diversity and latent-distribution analyses. Across all fractions, no augmentation variant significantly outperforms real-data-only training, and the quantum and classical generators are statistically indistinguishable. Any low-data benefit behaves as regularization rather than faithful data expansion:synthetic samples are off distribution and severely mode collapsed precisely where data is scarce, and the quantum generator is no more diverse thanits classical counterpart. We release the protocol as a testbed for rigorous evaluation of quantum generative augmentation in medical imaging.

</details>


### [73] [Low-Cost Neuromorphic Fall Detection Using Synthetic Event Data and Hybrid SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18732)
*Guillermo Rojas,Gonzalo Soto,Daniel Yunge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了融合脉冲神经网络(SNN)与卷积神经网络(CNN)的混合模型，通过将常规手机视频转化为模拟事件相机(DVS)数据进行人体跌倒检测，在保持精度的同时显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用SNN的能效优势和时空处理能力，结合DVS事件相机技术，解决传统跌倒检测系统在真实环境中功耗高、精度不足的问题。

Method: 将常规智能手机视频帧转换为模拟DVS事件数据，构建SNN-CNN混合模型，通过多种数据集模拟评估，并与传统机器学习模型进行对比。

Result: 混合模型在保持与传统方法相当精度的前提下，实现了显著的效率提升。

Conclusion: SNN与DVS技术的结合在真实环境的复杂任务中具有巨大潜力，能够在不牺牲准确性的情况下大幅提高能效。

Abstract: This work presents the development of hybrid models that integrate spiking neural networks (SNNs) with components of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) to learn from simulated event-based camera data (Dynamic Vision Sensor, DVS) generated from conventional smartphone videos. Aimed primarily at human fall detection, the approach leverages the energy efficiency and spatio-temporal processing capabilities of SNNs by converting video frames into event-based data. The proposed models are evaluated through simulations on multiple datasets, comparing their performance to that of traditional machine learning models. Results demonstrate significant gains in efficiency without sacrificing accuracy, underscoring the potential of combining SNNs and DVS technology for complex tasks in real-world environments.

</details>


### [74] [RouteJudge: An Open Platform for Reproducible and Preference-Aware LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18774)
*Guannan Lai,Haoran Hu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RouteJudge是面向LLM路由系统的在线成对偏好评估框架，专注于路由级决策质量评估；配套发布ORBIT工具箱，支持路由算法的标准化开发与在线评估提交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级响应评估无法有效衡量路由系统的决策质量，缺乏面向路由策略本身的偏好评估框架，且路由方法缺乏统一的开发和评测协议。

Method: RouteJudge通过匿名成对比较呈现不同路由策略选择的模型响应，将用户偏好归因于背后的路由策略；ORBIT提供统一的基准加载、查询表示、路由实现、预算感知评估和方法比较接口。

Result: 构建了在线偏好评估平台，存储包含查询、路由决策、模型响应、偏好标签、成本、延迟及任务元数据的完整评估记录，支持偏好感知、成本感知和任务条件化的路由分析。

Conclusion: RouteJudge实现了路由级决策质量评估，ORBIT标准化了LLM路由的端到端工作流，支持路由算法的持续扩展和在线评估。

Abstract: We present RouteJudge, an online pairwise preference evaluation framework for LLM routing systems, with a public platform available at https://routejudge.cn. Different from model-level response evaluation, RouteJudge focuses on router-level decision quality. For each user query, multiple routing strategies independently recommend candidate models under the same model pool and budget constraints. The selected model responses are then presented to users through anonymous pairwise comparisons, and the resulting user preferences are attributed back to the routing strategies behind the compared responses. Each evaluation record stores the query, routing decisions, model responses, preference labels, cost, latency, and task metadata, enabling preference-aware, cost-aware, and task-conditioned analysis of LLM routers. To support the continuous expansion of routing methods in RouteJudge, we further release ORBIT (Optimal Routing and Budgeted Inference Toolbox), a modular and extensible toolbox that standardizes the end-to-end workflow of LLM routing. ORBIT provides unified interfaces for benchmark loading, query representation, router implementation, budget-aware evaluation, and method comparison, allowing researchers to develop and evaluate routing algorithms under consistent protocols. It also serves as the submission and integration layer for RouteJudge: researchers can implement routing methods within ORBIT, validate them on existing routing benchmarks, and submit compatible routers for online preference-based evaluation. The code of ORBIT is available at https://github.com/AIGNLAI/LAMDA-ORBIT.

</details>


### [75] [A Hybrid LSTM--Vision Transformer Architecture for Predicting HRRR Forecast Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19026)
*David Aaron Evans,Jay C. Rothenberger,Kara J. Sulia,Nick P. Bassill,Chris D. Thorncroft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合LSTM-Vision Transformer（LSTM-ViT）框架，结合地面观测时序学习与大气廓线数据，用于预测高分辨率数值天气预报（HRRR）的预报误差。相比基线LSTM，该框架在降水、10米风速和2米温度预报误差预测上均有提升，尤其在短预报时效和边界层活动增强期间表现显著，降水误差预测技能提升约两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率NWP系统的预报误差常与未解析的边界层过程、对流、地形诱导环流等垂直结构大气现象相关。前期研究表明LSTM可利用地面观测预测HRRR预报误差，但性能在复杂垂直大气演化期间下降，亟待改进。

Method: 开发混合LSTM-ViT框架：LSTM组件学习地面观测的时间序列特征，Vision Transformer组件编码纽约州中尺度网廓线仪获取的大气垂直廓线信息，两者融合后预测HRRR模式的小时降水、10米风速和2米温度预报误差。

Result: 相比基线LSTM，LSTM-ViT在所有三个预报变量上均提升了预报误差预测技能；短预报时效和边界层活动增强期间改进最大；降水预报误差预测技能提升约两倍，能更好捕捉对流驱动的误差演变并减少边界层过程导致的性能退化。

Conclusion: 将时序学习与垂直注意力机制相结合，为改善业务NWP系统的预报误差预测提供了一条物理上有意义的途径，可为预报员提供关于模式偏差和预报置信度的增强指导。

Abstract: Forecast errors in high-resolution numerical weather prediction (NWP) systems are often linked to unresolved planetary boundary layer (PBL) processes, convection, terrain-induced circulations, and other vertically structured atmospheric phenomena. Previous work demonstrated that Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks can successfully predict forecast errors in the High-Resolution Rapid Refresh (HRRR) model using mesonet observations, but we believe performance degradation is linked to periods of complex vertical atmospheric evolution. To address this limitation, we develop a hybrid LSTM-Vision Transformer (LSTM-ViT) framework that combines temporal sequence learning from surface observations with atmospheric profiles from the New York State Mesonet profiler network. The LSTM-ViT framework is trained to predict HRRR hourly precipitation, 10 m wind speed, and 2 m temperature forecast errors at individual mesonet stations. Across all three predictors, incorporation of profiler-derived atmospheric structure improves forecast error prediction skill relative to the baseline LSTM architecture, with the largest gains occurring at shorter forecast lead times and during periods of enhanced PBL activity. Improvements are particularly pronounced for precipitation forecast error, where the LSTM-ViT framework achieves approximately a twofold increase in predictive skill relative to the baseline LSTM while better capturing convectively driven error evolution and reducing degradation associated with PBL processes. These results demonstrate that combining temporal sequence learning with vertically informed attention mechanisms provides a physically meaningful pathway for improving forecast error prediction in operational NWP systems. Our research offers forecasters enhanced guidance regarding model bias and forecast confidence.

</details>


### [76] [Online Distributional Prediction via Latent Cluster Geometry Under Drift and Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18778)
*Navyansh Mahla,Prateek Chanda,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可逆跳跃MCMC采样的吉布斯拟后验在线分布预测方法，通过潜变量聚类几何结构对非平稳流数据进行建模，并在有漂移和对抗性破坏的情况下实现了次线性Wasserstein累积遗憾的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线学习关注点估计的跟踪，但许多应用需要预测完整的数据生成分布。现有方法在处理非平稳流中的分布漂移和对抗性破坏时缺乏结构化的潜空间来量化不确定性、进行正则化和比较。

Method: 1) 将每个候选分布表示为可变大小的中心配置（潜聚类几何），组织概率质量并诱导预测分布；2) 构建吉布斯拟后验，通过后验平均生成在线预测器；3) 使用可逆跳跃MCMC采样可变维后验；4) 针对漂移提出重启变体，在时间上局部化拟贝叶斯更新。

Result: 高概率界分解为三个项：PAC-贝叶斯复杂度项、对破坏敏感的后验扰动项、由A_T^OT驱动的动态最优传输项。在有界支撑、潜几何稳定、预测映射正则、oracle可实现、重启窗口局部化、次线性传输作用和次线性破坏预算条件下，重启预测器实现次线性累积Wasserstein遗憾。

Conclusion: 该方法无需对数据流、漂移机制或破坏过程指定参数模型，同时保留了结构化潜空间以处理不确定性、正则化和比较，并在理论上提供了非渐进保证。

Abstract: Online learning in non-stationary streams is often formulated as tracking a point estimate, but many applications require predicting the full data-generating distribution. We study online distributional prediction under drift and adversarial corruption. Our approach represents each candidate law through a latent cluster geometry: a variable-size configuration of centers that organizes probability mass and induces a predictive distribution. A Gibbs quasi-posterior over these configurations yields an online predictor by posterior averaging, and the resulting variable-dimensional posterior can be sampled with reversible-jump MCMC. The method therefore avoids specifying a parametric streaming law while retaining a structured latent space for uncertainty, regularization, and comparison.
  We evaluate performance by cumulative Wasserstein-1 regret against the time-varying true law. The analysis separates two effects: corruption perturbs the loss-based posterior update, whereas drift makes long-horizon posterior memory stale. We address the latter with a restarted variant that temporally localizes the same quasi-Bayesian update. The resulting high-probability bounds decompose into a PAC-Bayesian complexity term, a corruption-sensitive posterior perturbation term, and a dynamic optimal-transport term driven by \(A_T^{\mathrm{OT}}=\sum_{t=2}^T W_2^2(p_{t-1}^*,p_t^*)\). Under bounded support, stable latent geometry, predictive-map regularity, oracle realizability, localized restart windows, sublinear transport action, and sublinear corruption budget, the restarted predictor achieves sublinear cumulative Wasserstein regret. These guarantees require no parametric model for the stream, drift mechanism, or corruption process.

</details>


### [77] [Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19134)
*Arnaud Lequen,Clément Legrand-Lixon,Léo Saulières*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PQLRM是一种结合Pareto Q-Learning与奖励机结构的的多目标强化学习算法，能高效学习非马尔可夫奖励下的Pareto最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如PQL在处理非马尔可夫奖励时样本效率低，而QRM只能学习单一最优策略，无法处理多目标权衡问题。PQLRM旨在结合两者优势，实现多目标场景下高效且能逼近Pareto前沿的学习。

Method: 将Pareto Q-Learning (PQL) 与 Q-Learning with Reward Machines (QRM) 相结合：利用奖励机自动机结构分解奖励信号，并维护向量形式的Q估计集合以逼近Pareto前沿，从而在非马尔可夫奖励下实现多策略学习。

Result: 实验表明PQLRM比在交叉积MDP上直接应用PQL收敛更快，并且能够合成QRM无法获得的Pareto最优策略。

Conclusion: PQLRM有效融合了PQL的多目标优化能力和QRM的结构化奖励利用能力，在多目标非马尔可夫奖励场景下优于两种基线方法。

Abstract: We present Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines (PQLRM), a multi-objective reinforcement learning algorithm for tasks whose reward structure is specified by a set of reward machines (RMs). PQLRM combines Pareto Q-Learning (PQL), which maintains sets of vector-valued Q-estimates to approximate the Pareto front, with enhancements from Q-Learning with Reward Machines (QRM), which exploits the factored automaton structure of the reward signal. This yields a multi-policy algorithm that remains sample-efficient under non-Markovian, RM-encoded rewards. Experimental trials show that PQLRM converges faster than a naive PQL baseline applied to the cross-product MDP and can synthesize Pareto-optimal policies that QRM cannot.

</details>


### [78] [OrthoReg: Orthogonal Regularization for Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19145)
*Till Richter,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对混合建模中神经网络可能重复学习物理机制部分的问题，提出正交正则化方法OrthoReg，通过直接惩罚符号组件与神经组件之间的重叠，实现互补分解，提升符号恢复和分布外表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合建模中，神经组件可能重新学习机械部分，产生冗余且不可解释的模型，尤其在符号结构本身是从数据中发现时。现有基于L2正则化的方法在符号组件通过稀疏发现学习时会失效，无法防止神经增强与符号结构重叠。

Method: 提出OrthoReg（正交正则化），直接惩罚符号组件与神经组件之间的重叠，防止符号结构被神经残差吸收，实现互补分解：符号部分捕获库可表达的内容，神经部分捕获剩余部分。

Result: 在具有部分库不匹配的基准动力系统上，OrthoReg改善了符号恢复和分布外行为。

Conclusion: OrthoReg通过在符号和神经组件间施加正交约束，有效解决了混合建模中的冗余学习问题，实现了更好的可解释性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Dynamical systems are fundamental to modeling the natural world, yet modeling them involves a persistent trade-off: manually prescribed mechanistic models are interpretable by design but often overly simplistic and misspecified; in contrast, flexible data-driven neural methods lack physical insight. Hybrid modeling aims for the best of both worlds by combining a prescribed or symbolic, physics-based component with a flexible neural network. A critical challenge, however, is that the neural component may relearn mechanistic parts, yielding redundant and uninterpretable models, especially when the symbolic structure itself is discovered from data. Existing methods based on standard $L^2$ regularization rely on a projection argument that breaks when the symbolic component is learned through sparse discovery, allowing the neural augmentation to overlap with symbolic structure. We introduce \textbf{OrthoReg} (Orthogonal Regularization), which directly penalizes overlap between the symbolic and neural components, preventing symbolic structure from being absorbed by the neural residual. This yields a complementary decomposition: the symbolic part captures what the library can express, and the neural part captures what remains. On benchmark dynamical systems with partial library mismatch, OrthoReg improves symbolic recovery and out-of-distribution behavior.

</details>


### [79] [Essential Subspace Merging for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19164)
*Longhua Li,Lei Qi,Xin Geng,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于本质子空间分解（ESD）的模型合并方法，通过将任务更新分解到激活偏移的主成分方向上，实现训练无关的静态合并（ESM）和动态合并（ESM++），有效降低多任务间的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并面临多任务间的参数更新相互干扰问题，现有方法未能有效区分任务相关方向与干扰方向，导致合并模型性能下降。

Method: 提出本质子空间分解（ESD），根据激活偏移的主成分分解每个任务更新。基于此提出ESM（静态合并方法，正交化并融合本质分量）和ESM++（动态合并方法，将任务残差分解为低秩专家，通过原型路由选择最相关专家）。

Result: 多组实验表明，ESM和ESM++在多种任务集和模型规模下有效保留任务知识并减少任务间干扰。

Conclusion: 利用任务更新能量集中在少数主方向（本质子空间）这一观察，通过分解与正交化处理可显著提升模型合并效果，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Model merging aims to enable multi-task learning by integrating the capabilities of multiple models fine-tuned from the same pre-trained checkpoint into a single model. Its core challenge is inter-task interference among task-specific parameter updates. In this paper, we analyze the output shifts induced by task updates and observe that their energy is concentrated in a small number of principal directions. We call the subspace spanned by these directions the essential subspace. In contrast, most remaining directions carry little task-relevant energy, but their accumulation across multiple task updates can cause severe interference during merging. Motivated by this observation, we propose Essential Subspace Decomposition (ESD), which decomposes each task update according to the principal components of its activation shift. Based on ESD, we introduce Essential Subspace Merging (ESM), a training-free static merging method that orthogonalizes and fuses essential components into one compact multi-task model. We further extend ESM to ESM++, a training-free dynamic merging method that decomposes task-specific residuals into low-rank experts and selects the most relevant expert through prototype-based routing during forward inference. Extensive experiments across multiple task sets and model scales demonstrate that ESM and ESM++ effectively preserves task knowledge while reducing inter-task interference.

</details>


### [80] [Compute Efficiency and Serial Runtime Tradeoffs for Stochastic Momentum Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19179)
*Depen Morwani,Alexandru Meterez,Pranav Nair,Sham Kakade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机动量方法（HB、Nesterov动量、ASGD）在一致性线性回归中的批量大小权衡，证明了HB在任意谱下不改善SGD的CE前沿，但允许更大批量窗口（约√κ倍）以减少序列运行时间；ASGD的CE-序列权衡则依赖于谱衰减速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机动量方法（如HB、Nesterov动量、ASGD）广泛用于现代训练，但其随机效益取决于序列运行时间（迭代次数）和计算效率（CE，梯度查询或FLOP成本的倒数）两个量。更大的批量可以在不损害CE的前提下减少序列运行时间，但这要求收缩间隙随批量大小线性增长。作者希望从理论上理解动量方法在批量大小权衡上的行为。

Method: 针对具有高斯协变量的一致性线性回归，研究随机HB和ASGD。在有限维离散时间设定下，推导批量大小权衡的下界。通过理论分析比较HB、ASGD与SGD在不同谱分布下的CE前沿和序列运行时间，并通过合成线性回归实验验证。

Result: 1) HB不改善SGD的CE前沿，但能在更大批量窗口（约√κ倍于SGD临界批量大小）内保持SGD级别的CE，从而减少序列运行时间，直到达到确定性加速尺度。2) ASGD的CE-序列权衡依赖于谱衰减速度：快速衰减幂律谱下，ASGD在小批量时改善CE；随批量增大，以CE优势换取序列运行时间改善。3) 实验验证了定性 regimes，包括慢衰减谱下ASGD和HB接近重叠，以及快速衰减谱下预测的CE-序列权衡。

Conclusion: 随机动量方法在大批量场景下的效益依赖于谱结构：HB不改进CE前沿但扩大批量窗口，ASGD则在小批量下改善CE但随批量增大面临权衡。这些理论结果为实际中根据数据谱特性选择动量方法和批量大小提供了指导。

Abstract: Stochastic momentum methods such as heavy ball (HB), Nesterov momentum, and variants of Accelerated SGD (ASGD) [Kidambi et al., 2018] are widely used in modern training, but their stochastic benefits depend on two distinct quantities: serial runtime, the number of iterations needed to reach a target accuracy, and compute efficiency (CE), the inverse total gradient-query or FLOP cost. Larger batches reduce serial runtime without hurting CE only when the contraction gap grows linearly with batch size.
  We study stochastic HB and ASGD for consistent linear regression with Gaussian covariates and prove finite-dimensional, discrete-time lower bounds on their batch-size tradeoffs. Our first result shows that HB does not improve the CE frontier over SGD for arbitrary spectra; rather, it preserves SGD-level CE over a larger batch-size window, allowing larger batches to reduce serial runtime until HB reaches its deterministic accelerated scale. This window can be a factor $\sqrtκ$ larger than the SGD critical batch size. For ASGD, the picture is more spectrum-dependent: for rapidly decaying power-law spectra, ASGD improves small-batch CE over HB/SGD, but as batch size grows it trades this CE advantage for improved serial runtime. Synthetic linear-regression experiments verify these qualitative regimes, including near-overlap of ASGD and HB for slowly decaying spectra and the predicted CE--serial tradeoff for rapidly decaying spectra.

</details>


### [81] [Forecasting what Matters: Decision-Focused RL for Controlled EV Charging with Unknown Departure Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19199)
*Giuseppe Gabriele,Fabio Pavirani,Seyed Soroush Karimi Madahi,Chris Develder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出决策聚焦强化学习（DF-RL）框架，通过端到端联合训练预测器（预测EV离开时间）与RL充电控制器，相较于传统分离训练方法，将未供应能量降低55%，总奖励提升14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车普及导致电网峰值负荷增加和稳定性问题；现有RL充电策略依赖关键特征（如离开时间），但实际场景中这些特征常不可得；传统预测器仅以精度为目标训练，其误差会向下游控制器传播，降低决策质量。

Method: 提出决策聚焦RL（DF-RL）框架：将预测器与RL充电控制器进行端到端联合训练，使预测器接收来自RL代理动作的反馈信号，而非仅以预测精度为优化目标。

Result: 与无离开时间预测的RL方法相比，DF-RL方法总奖励提升14%，未供应能量（因EV提前离开而未能完成的充电量）降低55%。

Conclusion: 决策聚焦的端到端训练范式能有效缓解预测误差在控制流水线中的传播问题，相比分离式训练产生更高质量的充电决策。

Abstract: The recent growth of EV adoption poses challenges for power systems, including increased peak demand and potential grid instability. Smart control of EV charging -- e.g., based on reinforcement learning (RL) -- can alleviate these issues by learning temporal and contextual patterns from historical data. Yet, in real-world scenarios, key features, such as departure time, often are unavailable. This, in turn, makes it harder for an RL agent to learn and execute an effective charging policy. To mitigate this uncertainty, a trained forecaster can approximate the unknown features from available data. However, since these forecasting models are typically trained for accuracy (rather than their impact on a downstream agent's decision quality), their errors may propagate and hinder the overall performance of a controller that is using the forecasts. To avoid this, we propose a decision-focused RL (DF-RL) framework in which the forecaster is trained end-to-end, i.e., with feedback from the charging policy actions taken by the RL agent. Such joint training of both the forecaster and controller ultimately results in higher-quality actions: our proposed DF-RL method yields superior charging decisions compared to other baselines, achieving up to a 14% improvement in total reward and a 55% reduction of unsupplied energy (i.e., charging that failed to happen because the EV already left), relative to the RL method without departure time forecasting.

</details>


### [82] [GateMem: Benchmarking Memory Governance in Multi-Principal Shared-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18829)
*Zhe Ren,Yibo Yang,Yimeng Chen,Zijun Zhao,Benshuo Fu,Zhihao Shu,Bingjie Zhang,Yangyang Xu,Dandan Guo,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GateMem是一个面向多主体共享记忆场景的基准测试，评估LLM智能体在医疗、办公、教育、家庭等领域的效用、访问控制和主动遗忘能力。实验表明，现有方法无法同时实现强效用、鲁棒访问控制和可靠遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆基准测试多假设单用户场景，忽略了医院、工作场所、校园和家庭等共享助手场景中多主体共同读写记忆池的复杂需求，需要同时评估记忆质量中的治理能力和回忆能力。

Method: GateMem构建了跨医疗、办公、教育、家庭四个领域的多主体共享记忆基准，包含长篇幅多轮对话、增量记忆注入、隐藏检查点、结构化评判和泄露目标标注，同时评估长程任务效用、上下文授权边界访问控制和删除请求后的主动遗忘。

Result: 多种基线和骨干模型测试显示，没有方法能同时实现强效用、鲁棒访问控制和可靠遗忘。长上下文提示方法在治理分数上最优但令牌成本高，检索和外部记忆方法成本低但会泄露未授权或被删除的信息。

Conclusion: 当前记忆智能体远未达到可靠的共享机构部署要求，需要进一步研究以平衡效用、访问控制和遗忘三者之间的关系。

Abstract: Memory benchmarks for LLM agents largely assume single-user settings, leaving shared assistants for hospitals, workplaces, campuses, and households understudied. In these deployments, multiple principals write to a common memory pool and query it under different roles, scopes, and relationships, so memory quality requires governance as well as recall. We introduce GateMem, a benchmark for multi-principal shared-memory agents. GateMem jointly evaluates utility for legitimate long-horizon requests with state updates, access control across contextual authorization boundaries, and agent-facing active forgetting after explicit deletion requests. It spans medical, office, education, and household domains, with long-form multi-party episodes, incremental memory injection, hidden checkpoints, structured judging, and leak-target annotations. Across diverse baselines and backbone models, no method simultaneously achieves strong utility, robust access control, and reliable forgetting. Long-context prompting often yields the best governance score at high token cost, while retrieval-based and external-memory methods reduce cost yet still leak unauthorized or deleted information. These results show current memory agents remain far from reliable shared institutional deployment.

</details>


### [83] [Machine Unlearning for the XGBoost Model with Network Intrusion Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19220)
*Diana Magalhães,Eva Maia,João Vitorino,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了XGBoost-Forget方法，针对XGBoost模型在网络入侵检测表格数据上的机器遗忘问题，实验表明该方法在保持模型性能的同时显著提升了遗忘效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘研究主要聚焦于深度学习和图像数据，而网络入侵检测领域大量依赖表格数据，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出了XGBoost-Forget遗忘方法，在两个表格网络入侵数据集（IoT-23和GeNIS）上进行评估，使用多个指标衡量模型性能、遗忘效率和遗忘质量。

Result: XGBoost-Forget在保持与原始模型相近的预测性能的同时，实现了显著更快的遗忘速度。

Conclusion: XGBoost-Forget展示了在表格网络入侵场景中应用机器遗忘的潜力，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning (MU) has emerged as an important technique for removing specific data points from trained models without requiring full retraining. However, most existing MU research focuses on deep learning and image data, leaving a gap in the domain of network intrusion detection, which relies heavily on tabular data. This work introduces XGBoost-Forget, an unlearning approach for the XGBoost model, to address this gap. The approach is evaluated on two tabular Network Intrusion (NI) datasets, IoT-23 and GeNIS, using multiple metrics to assess model performance, unlearning efficiency, and forgetting quality. The results show that XGBoost-Forget maintains predictive performance close to the original model while providing significantly faster unlearning, demonstrating its potential for MU in tabular NI settings.

</details>


### [84] [Mechanism-Guided Selective Unlearning for RLVR-Induced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19222)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAST是一种机制对齐的选择性目标遗忘方法，通过仅更新注意力投影张量的顶层子集，在遗忘RLVR诱导的推理能力时显著降低附带损害，相比全参数更新在保持模型性能方面具有明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准全参数梯度上升方法在遗忘RLVR推理能力时会严重损害模型保留的数学推理能力（如MATH和GSM8K），需要一种更精确、附带损害更低的遗忘方法。

Method: MAST通过三个指标（离主能量、更新幅度、遗忘梯度耦合幅度）对注意力投影张量进行排序，然后仅更新排名最高的子集，而非全参数更新。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B上，MAST实现显著的目标遗忘（MATH forget从45/150降至37/150，McNemar p=0.0078），同时保持GSM8K（+0.8pp）和MATH retain（-0.5pp）。优势在多种种子、NPO/SimNPO目标及Qwen3上均能复现。

Conclusion: MAST提供了一种机制对齐的参数选择策略，比全参数更新更有效地平衡了目标遗忘与知识保留，在RLVR推理遗忘任务中具有显著优势。

Abstract: We propose MAST (Mechanism-Aligned Selective Targeting), a mechanism-guided method for unlearning RLVR-induced reasoning with substantially lower collateral damage than standard full-parameter updates. In matched SFT/RLVR checkpoints on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and Qwen3-1.7B-Base, the SFT-to-RLVR increment differs sharply from the SFT update in token-level delta-log-probability, and full-parameter gradient ascent forgets only by damaging retain MATH and GSM8K. MAST ranks attention-projection tensors by off-principal energy, update magnitude, and forget-gradient coupling magnitude, then updates only the top-ranked subset. On the primary model, MAST induces statistically significant target forgetting (MATH forget 45/150 to 37/150; McNemar p=0.0078) while preserving GSM8K (+0.8 pp) and MATH retain (-0.5 pp). The advantage reproduces across seeds, NPO/SimNPO objectives, and Qwen3, where MAST preserves GSM8K while full-parameter unlearning collapses it.

</details>


### [85] [Seed-Guided Semi-Supervised Clustering by A-Contrario Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18833)
*Nassir Mohammad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于统计对偶性的半监督聚类框架，通过将聚类视为异常检测的对偶问题，利用a-contrario统计推理和格式塔邻近性原理，在无需手动参数调优的情况下实现鲁棒聚类。核心的Perception算法使用期望阈值（E<1）识别异常点，并通过"聚类-排除"迭代机制扩展种子簇，线性可扩展至观测数和维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决噪声环境下鲁棒聚类定义的挑战——划分算法容易过度分配离群点，而密度方法对启发式全局参数敏感。现有方法缺乏原则性的自动参数选择机制。

Method: 基于a-contrario统计推理和格式塔邻近性原理，将聚类定义为相对于均匀随机性零假设不包含任何异常点的最大数据子集。Perception算法利用期望阈值（E<1）自动识别离群点，采用种子引导的"聚类-排除"迭代机制：利用少量用户标签初始化鲁棒簇中心并形成初始分组，然后通过接纳非异常点扩展簇。

Result: 在合成和真实基准（包括原始、线性降维和邻域保持嵌入表示的图像和文本数据集）上，仅需每类10-30个种子即可达到有竞争力甚至很强的性能，且保持线性可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出的半监督聚类框架在低调优基准测试协议下表现出色，能自然分离边缘点、孤立噪声和新出现的未知簇，兼顾鲁棒性与效率。

Abstract: This paper introduces a semi-supervised clustering framework grounded in the statistical duality between grouping principles and anomaly detection. We address the challenge of robust cluster definition in noisy environments -- a task where partitioning algorithms often over-assign outliers and density-based methods remain sensitive to heuristic global parameters. Drawing on \textit{a-contrario} statistical reasoning and Gestalt proximity principles, we define a cluster as a maximal subset of data points containing no anomalies relative to a null hypothesis of uniform randomness. Central to this approach is the Perception algorithm, which utilises a principled expectation-based threshold ($\mathbb{E} < 1$) to identify outliers without manual parameter tuning. By treating clustering as the dual of anomaly detection, we employ an iterative ``clustering-by-exclusion'' mechanism. The algorithm is seed-guided, leveraging minimal user-provided labels to initialise robust cluster medians and form initial groups, which are subsequently expanded by admitting non-anomalous points. This approach naturally isolates fringe points, isolated noise, and emerging unknown clusters. We evaluate the method on synthetic and real-world benchmarks, including image and text datasets represented through raw, linear-reduced, and neighbourhood-preserving embeddings. Results demonstrate that with as few as 10--30 seeds per cluster, the proposed method achieves competitive and often very strong performance under a practical low-tuning benchmarking protocol, while maintaining linear scalability with respect to both observations and dimensionality for a fixed number of seeded clusters and iterations.

</details>


### [86] [STARE: Surprisal-Guided Token-Level Advantage Reweighting for Policy Entropy Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19236)
*Haipeng Luo,Qingfeng Sun,Songli Wu,Can Xu,Wenfeng Deng,Han Hu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STARE方法，通过基于惊异度的Token级优势重加权和闭环熵门控机制，解决GRPO在LLM推理强化学习中的策略熵坍塌问题，在1.5B-32B模型上稳定训练数千步，AIME24/25上超越DAPO等基线4%-8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等基于可验证奖励的强化学习算法在LLM复杂推理后训练中常遭遇策略熵坍塌，导致探索能力丧失，亟需一种能稳定控制策略熵的方法。

Method: 首先对GRPO进行Token级熵动力学的梯度分析，发现Token级信用分配不匹配问题；提出STARE方法：通过批量内惊异度分位数识别熵关键Token子集，选择性重加权其有效优势，并引入目标熵闭环门控进行稳定熵调节。

Result: 在1.5B至32B的多个模型规模上，覆盖Short CoT、Long CoT和Multi-Turn Tool Use三种任务，STARE能在数千步训练中保持策略熵在目标带内；在AIME24和AIME25上，平均准确率超越DAPO等基线4%-8%，反思Token和响应长度同步增长。

Conclusion: STARE通过Token级惊异度引导的优势重加权有效缓解了熵坍塌问题，维持了探索-利用平衡，进一步释放了RL训练潜力，在多个规模和任务上取得一致提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards algorithms like GRPO have emerged as the dominant post-training paradigm for complex reasoning in LLMs, yet commonly suffer from policy entropy collapse during training. We conduct a first-order gradient analysis of token-level entropy dynamics under GRPO and identify a token-level credit assignment mismatch: the per-token entropy variation decomposes into the product of the trajectory-level advantage and an entropy sensitivity function over the next-token distribution, yielding an advantage-surprisal four-quadrant structure and a near-criticality property. Motivated by it, we propose STARE (Surprisal-guided Token-level Advantage Reweighting for policy Entropy stability), which identifies entropy-critical token subsets via batch-internal surprisal quantiles, selectively reweights their effective advantages, and incorporates a target-entropy closed-loop gate for stable entropy regulation. Across model scales from 1.5B to 32B and three task families (Short CoT, Long CoT, and Multi-Turn Tool Use), STARE sustains stable RL training over thousands of steps while maintaining policy entropy within the target band. On AIME24 and AIME25, STARE outperforms DAPO and other competitive baselines by 4%-8% in average accuracy, with reflection tokens and response length growing in tandem, indicating sustained exploration-exploitation balance that further unlocks RL training potential.Code is available at https://github.com/hp-luo/STARE.

</details>


### [87] [Identifying Structural Biases from Causal Mechanism Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18834)
*Praharsh Nanavati,Jilles Vreeken,David Kaltenpoth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过检测因果机制变化中的依赖关系来识别隐藏混淆和选择偏差，并基于互信息提出可检验准则，开发了StruBI算法，在合成和真实数据上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法通常假设数据独立同分布且无未测量变量，但这些假设在实际中常被违反，导致推断不准确。因此需要一种能从因果机制变化中识别隐藏混淆和选择偏差的方法。

Method: 通过分析不同环境下因果机制的变化，特别是结构偏差会导致机制变化产生依赖关系。基于互信息形式化了一个可经验检验的准则，并开发了StruBI算法来识别哪些节点受到何种偏差。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，StruBI能够准确恢复受影响的变量集和偏差类型，大幅优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 利用因果机制变化中的依赖关系可以有效识别隐藏混淆和选择偏差，为违反标准假设的因果发现提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Causal discovery methods commonly assume that all data is independently and identically distributed (i.i.d.) and that there are no unmeasured variables affecting the system. In practice, these assumptions are often violated, leading to inaccurate inference. In this paper, we study how to identify hidden confounding and selection biases from causal mechanism shifts. In particular, we show that structural biases lead to dependent mechanism shifts. That is, by considering for which variables the mechanisms change given data from different environments, we can tell which variables are unbiased, which are subject to hidden confounding, and which are undergoing selection bias. We formalize this into an empirically testable criterion based on mutual information, and show under which conditions it identifies structural biases. To tell which nodes are subject to what kind of bias, we introduce the StruBI algorithm. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that StruBI works well in practice, accurately recovering affected variable sets and types of biases, outperforming the state-of-the-art by a wide margin.

</details>


### [88] [Explaining Attention with Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19317)
*Amiri Hayes,Belinda Li,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将Transformer语言模型中的注意力头逆向工程为可执行的Python程序的方法，使用不到1000个程序即可重现GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B中注意力头的注意力模式（平均IoU>75%），替换25%的注意力头仅导致16%的困惑度增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长久以来，可解释深度学习的目标是将不透明的神经计算替换为人类可理解的符号描述，本文旨在通过将注意力头逆向工程为可执行程序，推动神经网络向符号透明性发展。

Method: 1) 对目标注意力头在随机训练样本上计算注意力矩阵；2) 使用预训练语言模型基于矩阵摘要生成能重现注意力模式的Python程序；3) 根据程序在保留样本上的表现进行重排序和筛选。

Result: 在GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B上，不到1000个生成程序即可重现注意力模式（平均IoU>75%）；替换25%的注意力头仅导致16%的平均困惑度增加，且在问答基准上保持性能。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一种可扩展的流水线，用于将Transformer注意力头逆向工程为人类可读的可执行代码，为神经网络实现符号透明性开辟了路径。

Abstract: A longstanding goal of research on interpretable deep learning is to replace opaque neural computations with human-meaningful symbolic descriptions. In this paper, we propose an approach for approximating the behavior of components of deep networks with executable programs. We focus on attention heads in transformer language models. For a given head, we first compute its associated attention matrices on a collection of randomly selected training examples. Next, we prompt a pre-trained language model with a summary of these matrices, and instruct it to generate a set of Python programs that can reproduce the associated attention patterns given only text from the input sentence. Finally, we re-rank programs according to how well our final set of programs predict behavior on held-out inputs. We demonstrate that a set of fewer than 1,000 such generated programs can reproduce the attention patterns of heads in GPT-2, TinyLlama-1.1B, and Llama-3B, achieving an average Intersection-over-Union similarity above 75% on TinyStories. Moreover, the best-fit programs can replace neural attention heads without substantially affecting model behavior: replacing 25% of attention heads with programmatic surrogates across the three models incurs only a 16% average perplexity increase, while maintaining performance on a variety of downstream question answering benchmarks. This work contributes a scalable pipeline for reverse-engineering attention heads in transformer models using human-readable, executable code, advancing a path toward symbolic transparency in neural models.

</details>


### [89] [Semantic Robustness Certification for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18839)
*Peiyu Yang,Paul Montague,Feng Liu,Andrew C. Cullen,Amardeep Kaur,Christopher Leckie,Sarah M. Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个无需额外数据即可对视觉语言模型（VLM）在语义层面变换（如形状、大小、风格）下进行鲁棒性认证的框架，通过利用文本提示作为语义代理，以闭式解刻画决策边界，定量认证预测类别不变的语义变化程度区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中VLM常面临由语义变化（形状、大小、风格等）引起的分布偏移，而现有鲁棒性认证工作主要针对几何或像素级变换，缺乏对语义级变换的认证方法。

Method: 利用VLM的开放词汇能力，以文本提示作为语义代理构建由程度参数控制的语义变换；通过闭式解刻画VLM决策边界，定量认证预测类别不变的语义变化区间。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，该框架能够在多种语义变化场景下实现鲁棒性认证。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了无需为每种变化额外收集数据即可认证VLM在语义级变换下的鲁棒性，具有良好的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are now widely used in downstream tasks. However, real-world applications often expose VLMs to distribution shifts induced by semantic variation (e.g., shape, size, and style). Robustness certification determines if a model's prediction changes when transformations are applied to its input. While most certification frameworks study geometric or pixel-level transformations over inputs, this work proposes a novel framework that enables certifying VLM robustness under semantic-level transformations. Leveraging the open-vocabulary capability of VLMs, we use text prompts as semantic proxies to construct transformations parameterized by an extent that controls the degree of semantic variation. By characterizing the VLM decision boundary in closed form, our framework quantitatively certifies extent intervals for which the predicted class remains unchanged under the semantic transformation. Our framework is the first to certify VLM robustness under semantic-level variations without requiring additional data for each variation, making it practical to apply. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world data show that our framework enables certifying robustness under diverse semantic variations across scenarios.

</details>


### [90] [UBP2: Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning for Efficient Preference-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19328)
*Mohamed Nabail,Leo Cheng,Jingmin Wang,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模型的不确定性平衡偏好规划方法(UBP2)，通过联合推理奖励、动力学和价值函数的不确定性，主动引导探索，在偏好强化学习中显著提升样本效率，并提供了理论保证和实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好强化学习方法依赖被动数据收集，样本效率低下，尤其在学习早期阶段表现不佳，缺乏主动探索机制。

Method: UBP2方法采用奖励、动力学和价值函数模型的集成，根据统一评分（结合期望奖励、终端价值和认知不确定性）评估候选轨迹，在规划目标下实现探索与利用的显式权衡，无需启发式探索策略。

Result: 在标准正则性假设下建立了有限时域和无限时域设置的亚线性遗憾界；在Meta-World基准实验上，UBP2相比无模型偏好方法和非乐观模型基基线方法取得了更高的样本效率。

Conclusion: UBP2通过联合不确定性推理实现了高效的主动探索，为偏好强化学习中的样本效率问题提供了理论与实践的解决方案。

Abstract: Preference-based RL provides an approach to learning reward models from pairwise comparisons of behaviors, bypassing the need for explicit reward design. However, existing methods typically rely on passive data collection and suffer from poor sample efficiency, especially during the early stages of learning. We introduce a model-based approach that actively directs exploration by jointly reasoning over uncertainties in the reward, dynamics, and value functions. Our method, Uncertainty-Balanced Preference Planning (UBP2), uses ensembles of reward, dynamics, and value function models to evaluate candidate trajectories according to a unified score that combines expected reward, terminal value, and epistemic uncertainty. Planning under this objective yields an explicit tradeoff between exploitation and information acquisition without requiring ad hoc exploration heuristics. Under standard regularity assumptions, we establish sublinear regret guarantees for both finite-horizon and infinite-horizon settings. Empirically, experiments on the Meta-World benchmark show UBP2 achieves substantially higher sample efficiency than model-free preference-based methods and non-optimistic model-based baselines.

</details>


### [91] [Learning from Your Own Mistakes: Constructing Learnable Micro-Reflective Trajectories for Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18844)
*Zhilin Huang,Hang Gao,Ziqiang Dong,Yuan Chen,Yifeng Luo,Chujun Qin,Jingyi Wang,Yang Yang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trajectory-Augmented Policy Optimization (TAPO)，将自蒸馏从隐式分布对齐推进到显式轨迹构建。TAPO 利用模型同时产生的正确和错误 rollout 构建微反射修正轨迹，保留错误推理至失败点，再插入自然语言诊断和修正推理。该方法在 AIME 2024/2025 和 HMMT 2025 上持续优于 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法通过隐式 logit 级对齐（最小化 KL 散度）利用模型自身的 rollout 作为训练信号，但监督信号来自无控制采样，无法提供诊断性洞察来识别模型的具体错误或纠正其个体失败模式，导致模型只是模仿特权分布而非获得细粒度纠错。

Method: TAPO 在 RL 训练中让模型对同一查询同时生成正确和错误的 rollout，利用对比结构构建微反射修正轨迹：保留模型错误推理至失败点，然后插入基于正确参考的自然语言诊断和修正推理。同时引入难度感知候选选择和去耦优势估计以防止梯度污染。

Result: 在 AIME 2024、AIME 2025 和 HMMT 2025 数据集上，TAPO 在相同训练步数下持续优于 GRPO。进一步分析表明 TAPO 同时增强了首次推理能力和纠错效果。

Conclusion: TAPO 通过显式轨迹构建替代隐式分布对齐，使自蒸馏过程能够提供细粒度的错误定位与修正信号，且由于每条轨迹锚定在学习者自身的前缀和解上，相比基于 KL 的位置对齐更好地保持了模型在策略分布。

Abstract: Self-distillation improves reasoning in large language models by using the model's own rollouts as training signal, typically through implicit logit-level alignment that minimizes KL divergence toward a privileged target distribution. However, because this supervision is generated via uncontrolled sampling, it provides no diagnostic insight into the model's specific errors or corrective guidance for its individual failure patterns. Consequently, the model learns to imitate a privileged distribution rather than receiving fine-grained corrections that pinpoint where and why its reasoning fails. In this paper, we propose Trajectory-Augmented Policy Optimization (TAPO), which advances self-distillation from implicit distributional alignment to explicit trajectory construction. During RL training, the model produces both correct and incorrect rollouts to the same query, and TAPO leverages this contrastive structure to construct micro-reflective corrections, new training trajectories that retain the model's erroneous reasoning up to the point of failure, then insert a natural-language diagnosis and corrected reasoning guided by a correct reference from the same sampling group. Since each trajectory is anchored in the learner's own prefix and solutions, the corrective signal preserves the model's on-policy distribution to a greater extent than the position-wise alignment imposed by KL-based methods. To integrate these trajectories, TAPO introduces difficulty-aware candidate selection at the model's capability boundary and decoupled advantage estimation to prevent gradient contamination. Experiments on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025 show that TAPO achieves consistent improvements over GRPO under the same number of training steps. Further analysis demonstrates that TAPO strengthens both first-pass reasoning and error-correction effectiveness.

</details>


### [92] [Investigating Inductive Biases for Machine Learning Emulation of Sudden Stratospheric Warmings in Idealised Isca Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18857)
*Oskar Bohn Lassen,Simon Driscoll,Stephen I. Thomson,Sebastian Schemm,Francisco C. Pereira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了机器学习仿真器在次季节到季节时间尺度天气预报中的应用，重点研究了不同架构（卷积、Transformer、图网络）对平流层突然增温（SSW）动力学的模拟能力。研究发现，当平流层活跃时，模型差异显著扩大；显式三维垂直耦合是关键归纳偏置，但低预测误差并不保证物理上准确的波-流相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习仿真器在天气预报中应用日益广泛，但能否利用平流层变率等可预测性锚点来超越短预报时效影响对流层环流，仍是一大挑战。本文旨在检验架构归纳偏置如何影响对SSW动力学的仿真能力。

Method: 使用配对理想化Isca模拟（仅改变施加的波-2加热扰动），对卷积、Transformer和图网络三种架构进行单步预测训练，比较它们在平流层静稳和活跃条件下的表现，并通过Eliassen-Palm通量诊断评估波-平均流相互作用的物理保真度。

Result: 平流层静稳时各架构差异较小，平流层活跃时差异显著增大。显式三维垂直耦合是关键归纳偏置。但低预测误差不能保证物理上准确的波-流相互作用，平流层波驱结构存在相干误差。

Conclusion: 机器学习仿真器需要引入显式三维垂直耦合来更好地模拟平流层动力学，同时仅依靠预测误差指标不足以评估模型的物理一致性，需结合物理诊断指标。

Abstract: Machine-learning emulators are increasingly used for weather prediction and have the potential to extend skill on subseasonal-to-seasonal timescales by learning dynamically important sources of predictability. A key challenge is whether the models can exploit predictability anchors, such as stratospheric variability, that influence tropospheric circulation beyond short lead times. We test how architectural inductive bias affects emulation of sudden stratospheric warming (SSW) dynamics using paired idealised Isca simulations that differ only in an imposed wave-2 heating perturbation. Across convolutional, transformer, and graph-based architectures trained for one-step prediction, model differences are modest when the stratosphere is dynamically quiet but widen substantially when SSW-like variability is active. Our results identify explicit three-dimensional vertical coupling as a key inductive bias for machine-learning emulation of stratospheric dynamics. However, Eliassen-Palm flux diagnostics show that low forecast error does not guarantee physically faithful wave-mean-flow interaction, with coherent errors remaining in stratospheric wave-driving structure.

</details>


### [93] [Strategic Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18867)
*Jivat Neet Kaur,Pratik Patil,Divya Shanmugam,Emma Pierson,Michael I. Jordan,Nika Haghtalab,Meena Jagadeesan,Ahmed Alaa,Serena Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在算法预测影响资源分配时，如何通过特征选择和岭正则化来应对策略性操纵。核心发现是仅根据可操纵性排除特征通常是次优的，并提出了联合优化特征集和正则化系数的实用算法。通过医疗支付案例验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域（如医疗），算法预测会引发输入特征的战略性操纵。现有方法需要重新设计预测器来显式考虑策略交互，但实践中决策者往往只能调整现有预测流水线中的粗粒度杠杆（如特征选择和正则化）。作者希望为这一受限场景提供理论指导和实用工具。

Method: 首先对特征选择与岭正则化交互下的策略分类问题进行形式化建模，给出了最优正则化下特征子集性能的精细刻画。基于该刻画，设计了联合优化特征集和正则化系数的实用算法，并在医疗支付基准数据上进行了案例研究。

Result: 仅依据可操纵性排除特征通常是次优的；提供了特征子集在最优正则化下性能的精细刻画；开发的联合优化算法在真实医疗支付数据上能有效指导粗粒度策略杠杆的设计。

Conclusion: 本文为通过特征选择和岭正则化缓解策略性行为提供了原则性的实用框架，填补了理论最优策略分类与实际操作约束之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: When algorithmic predictors inform resource allocation in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, these predictors must account for strategic manipulation of input features. The typical solution is to redesign the predictor itself to explicitly account for strategic interactions. In practice, however, decision makers are often constrained to adjusting coarser levers within existing prediction pipelines. For example, healthcare organizations often select which features to exclude based on perceived manipulability, while using standard regularization procedures to shrink the coefficients of retained features. In this work, we initiate a formal study of strategic classification through feature selection and its interaction with ridge regularization. Our main finding is that excluding individual features based on their manipulability alone is generally suboptimal. We provide a fine-grained characterization of the performance of a feature subset under optimal regularization, yielding new insights for policy design. Motivated by this characterization, we develop a practical algorithm for jointly choosing the feature set and the level of ridge regularization. Through a real-world case study on a healthcare payments benchmark, we illustrate how our algorithm can guide the design of coarse policy levers in practice. Our results provide a principled, practical framework for mitigating the effects of strategic behavior in algorithmic decision-making systems.

</details>


### [94] [Anomaly Detection for Sparse and Irregular Multivariate Time Series with Latent SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18898)
*Martin Uray,Dominik Geng,Florian Graf,Stefan Huber,Roland Kwitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于潜随机微分方程（Latent SDEs）的生成式方法，用于处理多变量时间序列异常检测中因缺失观测、不规则采样和稀疏数据导致的挑战，在六个基准数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTSAD方法假设时间序列均匀采样，而真实数据常存在稀疏、不规则采样或部分观测的问题，缺乏能自然处理这些不规则性的方法。

Method: 采用生成式方法，基于潜随机微分方程（Latent SDEs）将观测时间序列投影到连续时间随机动力系统上，可直接处理缺失观测和不规则采样，并自然捕捉真实数据中固有的循环行为。

Result: 在六个异常检测基准数据集上排名第一，超越现有基线方法；在严重数据稀疏场景下仍保持鲁棒性，而基线方法性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 潜SDE可作为多变量时间序列异常检测的自然归纳偏置，尤其适用于存在真实世界不规则性的场景。

Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD) is critical for a wide range of application areas, such as industrial monitoring, cybersecurity, or healthcare. Real-world data is often sparse, irregularly sampled or partially observed, yet existing methods assume uniformly sampled time series. We propose a generative approach based on Latent SDEs that projects the observed time series on a continuous-time stochastic dynamical system, directly being able to handle missing observations and irregular sampling, while also naturally capturing possible cyclic behavior that many real-world use cases inherently possess. Experiments on six anomaly benchmark datasets show that our proposed method ranks first among state-of-the-art baselines. We further demonstrate that our method remains robust under severe data sparsity, while performance significantly degrades for the tested baseline methods. These results highlight latent SDEs as a natural inductive bias for anomaly detection in multivariate time series, especially in presence of real-world irregularities.

</details>


### [95] [REVES: REvision and VErification--Augmented Training for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18910)
*Yuanxin Liu,Ruida Zhou,Xinyan Zhao,Amr Sharaf,Hongzhou Lin,Arijit Biswas,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Zhaoran Wang,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REVES，一种两阶段迭代框架，通过解耦中间步骤的修正与验证提示来对齐测试时多步推理，在LiveCodeBench上比RL基线高6.5分，4B模型达到圈堆积SOTA，并泛化到约束满足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准后训练方法优化单步目标，与多步推理动态存在根本性错位；现有多轮RL方法直接优化整个轨迹，未能利用中间步骤中的高质量错误供模型学习修正。

Method: 两阶段迭代框架：(1) 在线数据/提示增强阶段，将成功恢复轨迹中的中间步骤（"near-miss"答案）转化为解耦的修正与验证提示；(2) 策略优化阶段，聚焦于有效答案转换和错误识别训练。通过离策略数据生成降低长视野采样的计算开销。

Result: LiveCodeBench上比RL基线高+6.5分，比标准多轮训练高+4.0分；4B模型在圈堆积任务上匹配此前SOTA（使用更少 rollout）；数学推理修正能力提升；泛化到n_queens和mini_sudoku等约束满足问题。

Conclusion: 解耦中间步骤的修正与验证训练能够有效提升多步推理能力，在编码、数学和约束满足任务上均优于现有方法，且计算效率更高。

Abstract: Test-time scaling via sequential revision has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning. However, standard post-training methods primarily optimize single-shot objectives, creating a fundamental misalignment with multi-step inference dynamics. While recent work treats this as multi-turn reinforcement learning (RL), conventional approaches optimize over the multi-step trajectories directly, failing to further exploit the high-quality mistakes in intermediate steps that model can learn from correcting them. We propose a two-stage iterative framework that alternates between online data/prompt augmentation and policy optimization. By converting the intermediate steps (``near-miss'' answers) in the successful recovery trajectories into decoupled revision and verification prompts, our approach concentrates training on both effective answer transformation and error identification. This approach enables efficient off-policy data generation and reduces the computational overhead of long-horizon sampling compared to standard multi-turn RL. On LiveCodeBench, using publicly available test cases as feedback, we observe gains of +6.5 points over the RL baseline and +4.0 points over standard multi-turn training. Beyond coding, our approach matches the previously reported SOTA result on circle packing while using the smallest base model (4B) and far fewer rollouts than the much larger evolutionary search systems. Math results under ground-truth verification further confirm improved correction ability. It also generalizes to out-of-distribution constraint-satisfaction puzzles such as n\_queens and mini\_sudoku, where correctness is defined entirely by problem constraints. Code is available at https://github.com/yxliu02/REVES.git.

</details>


### [96] [Some Complexity Results for Robustness Verification for Binarized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18918)
*Harshit Goyal,Sudakshina Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了二值化神经网络（BNN）的可满足性问题与均匀图像遮挡下的鲁棒性验证问题的计算复杂度。证明BNN可满足性是NP完全的（通过从SAT归约），而均匀遮挡导致网络输出呈分段常数结构，从而可实现多项式时间的鲁棒性检查算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着BNN在资源受限场景中的广泛应用，其形式化验证的计算复杂度尚未被充分理解。本文旨在精确刻画BNN验证问题的计算边界，为实际部署中的可验证性提供理论依据。

Method: 采用计算复杂度理论中的归约方法：1) 将经典SAT问题多项式归约到BNN可满足性问题，证明NP-hardness；2) 分析均匀遮挡下网络输出的分段常数结构，利用该结构设计多项式时间算法。

Result: BNN可满足性问题是NP完全的；均匀遮挡下的鲁棒性验证可以在多项式时间内完成。

Conclusion: 本文清晰界定了BNN两类验证问题的计算复杂度边界：可满足性本质困难（NP完全），而特定形式的鲁棒性验证（均匀遮挡）具备高效算法，为BNN在实际应用中的可验证性提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper studies the computational complexity of verification problems for Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), where activations (and sometimes weights) are binary. We analyze two problems: satisfiability and robustness under uniform image occlusion. We show that BNN satisfiability is NP-complete via a reduction from Boolean satisfiability problem (SAT), and that uniform occlusion induces a piecewise-constant structure in the network output, enabling a polynomial-time robustness-checking algorithm.

</details>


### [97] [GrapNet: A Programmable Dynamic-Architecture Neural Graph Substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18923)
*Zirong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GrapNet提出将固定张量神经网络中的图结构作为一等可编程接口，支持关系编辑、子图冻结、局部函数审计和后端切换等操作，通过节点级分配向量和外部策略规则实现可编程性，在Split Fashion-MNIST和Split CIFAR-10上相比MLP基线取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前固定张量神经网络缺乏可编程性作为一等接口，编辑关系、冻结子图、审计局部函数或更改执行后端等操作需要对参数进行临时性手术，缺乏统一、可编程的图即网络范式。

Method: GrapNet将计算图视为神经网络架构和可执行程序（而非输入数据图）。每个计算节点拥有指向子层的引用和一个可训练分配向量。删除关系即物理移除子引用和对应分配坐标。结构规则和执行策略位于节点核心之外，支持子图生长、冻结、结构编辑、分组为可训练族块、注意力路由及拓扑稳定后下采样为密集快照。通过向量值父接口与CNN、ResNet、Transformer等常规模块组合。

Result: 在十次不同种子的Split Fashion-MNIST实验中，可塑GrapNet+ER头达到63.16%的已见类准确率，而参数更大的密集MLP+ER仅为51.08%，配对差值为12.08个点，p=1.3e-5。在Split CIFAR-10上（使用冻结的ImageNet ResNet-18编码器），同一基座相比MLP-256在线头提升3.81个点，p=0.0026。

Conclusion: GrapNet是一种可编辑的神经图基座，其核心价值在于结构可编程性，并提供忠实的执行视图。

Abstract: Programmability is a missing first-class interface in fixed-tensor neural networks: editing a relation, freezing a subgraph, auditing a local function, or changing the execution backend should be an operation on the neural program rather than ad-hoc parameter surgery. GrapNet studies this graph-as-network setting. The graph is the architecture and executable program, not an input data graph. Each compute node owns its next-layer child references and a trainable allocation vector aligned with those references; deleting a relation physically removes both the child reference and the corresponding allocation coordinate. Structural rules and execution policies live outside the node core, so the same child-owned graph can be grown, frozen, structurally edited, grouped into trainable family blocks, routed by attention over active relations, or lowered to dense snapshots after topology stabilizes. GrapNet composes with conventional modules through a vector-valued parent interface: dense layers, CNN encoders, ResNet feature extractors, attention blocks, and transformer representations can all feed one sensory GrapNode per coordinate. The evaluation is organized as a programmability stress suite rather than as a new replay benchmark. In a matched ten-seed Split Fashion-MNIST study, a plastic GrapNet+ER head reaches 63.16 percent seen-class accuracy versus 51.08 percent for a parameter-larger dense MLP+ER under the same seen-class loss and replay memory, with paired delta 12.08 points and p=1.3e-5. On Split CIFAR-10 with a frozen ImageNet ResNet-18 encoder, the same substrate improves the online head over MLP-256 by 3.81 points, with p=0.0026. These results support GrapNet as an editable neural graph substrate whose core value is structural programmability with faithful execution views.

</details>


### [98] [Zero-Shot Active Feature Acquisition via LLM-Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18933)
*Binyamin Perets,Natalie Mendelson,Shiran Vainberg,Yehuda Chowers,Shai Shen-Orr,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本主动特征获取（AFA）框架，通过有策略地引导大语言模型（LLM）提供马尔可夫随机场（MRF）的充分统计量（一元偏差和pairwise协变），避免LLM直接进行序列规划。采用最大熵闭包解决LLM仅返回判别性统计量导致的规范歧义问题。在炎症性肠病（IBD）患者数据集上，该方法在分类和Top-K识别任务中均优于LLM基线，尤其在最难患者样本上表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动特征获取方法依赖大量标注数据来拟合概率模型，而大语言模型虽具备无监督领域知识但缺乏序列规划能力。若让LLM同时承担知识提供和决策规划，会混淆本应分离的能力。因此需要一种框架将两者解耦，仅利用LLM可靠的知识提供能力。

Method: 1. 构建马尔可夫随机场（MRF）框架，将AFA问题转化为求解事件势函数的任务；2. 通过有策略地引导（disciplined elicitation）让LLM返回MRF的充分统计量：一元偏差（unary deviations）和pairwise协变（pairwise co-variations）；3. 针对LLM仅返回判别性统计量（区分性特征而非类内特征）导致的规范歧义（gauge ambiguity），采用最大熵闭包（maximum-entropy closure）解决；4. 应用于二分类和Top-K识别两个场景。

Result: 在IBD患者数据集上，该框架在真实标签和LLM自身抽取信念上均优于原始LLM。在最困难的患者的Top-K获取策略中，该方法显著优于所有现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的知识提供与序列规划能力解耦，利用MRF建模和最大熵闭包，可以在零样本条件下实现有效的主动特征获取，尤其适用于医疗等标注数据稀缺且需要处理复杂不确定性的场景。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) sequentially selects which features to observe to reach a classification or ranking decision. Its central limitation is reliance on large amount of labeled data to fit probabilistic models guiding acquisition. Large language models (LLMs) supply unsupervised domain knowledge, but are poor sequential planners. Asking one to both know and decide conflates capabilities best kept separate. Here, we develop a framework for zero-shot AFA through disciplined elicitation: asking the LLM only for what it can be trusted to return, the unary deviations and pairwise co-variations that are the sufficient statistics of a Markov random field (MRF). We apply our framework to two settings: binary classification and top-$k$ identification. In practice, the LLM reliably returns only discriminative statistics, what distinguishes the classes rather than each class in isolation, which precludes classical AFA. We apply a maximum-entropy closure that resolves this gauge ambiguity. We evaluate on a cohort of Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD) patients, an active clinical setting where diagnostic ambiguity and patient heterogeneity obstruct stable treatment strategies. Our framework outperforms the LLM both on real labels and on its own extracted beliefs. Where it matters most, on the hardest patients, our top-$k$ acquisition policy markedly outperforms all existing methods.

</details>


### [99] [Be Your Own Teacher: Steering Protein Language Models via Unsupervised Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18961)
*Lanqing Li,Shentong Mo,Yang Yu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督奖励优化的蛋白质语言模型框架（SRO/BRO），利用模型不确定性和语义一致性构建代理奖励，无需标注数据即可实现可控蛋白质生成，在组合分布外提示上显著优于DPO、KTO等基线，逼近oracle性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质语言模型的后训练适应依赖昂贵的湿实验验证或精心构建的偏好数据集，存在监督瓶颈。本文旨在消除对人工标注和实验反馈的依赖，实现无监督条件下的可控蛋白质设计。

Method: 提出无监督奖励优化框架：(1) 设计任务无关奖励函数，结合模型内在不确定性和基于蛋白质表征模型的外部语义一致性；(2) 提出两种离线优化算法——软奖励优化(SRO)和二值化奖励优化(BRO)，用于最大化基于代理奖励的类RLHF目标。

Result: 在组合分布外提示上，SRO和BRO显著优于DPO、KTO等基线，在不同采样温度、模型规模和蛋白质家族中均逼近oracle性能；无监督微调后的PLM在pass@k评估中实现比基础模型更高的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 提出了无需标注偏好或实验反馈的可扩展无监督奖励优化框架，使PLM能够通过自身生成的经验实现自我改进，为标注数据稀缺场景下的可控生物分子设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for controllable biomolecular design, yet their post-training adaptation typically relies on costly wet-lab validation or curated preference datasets. To overcome this supervision bottleneck, we introduce unsupervised reward optimization of PLMs, a comprehensive framework for steerable protein generation without ground-truth labels. Our key insight is that task-agnostic rewards, which combine intrinsic model uncertainty with extrinsic semantic consistency informed by protein representation models, exhibit strong correlation with controllability measures across base models and temperature regimes. Building upon this discovery, we propose two offline algorithms: Soft Reward Optimization (SRO) and Binarized Reward Optimization (BRO), which effectively maximize the classical RLHF objective induced by these proxy rewards. Extensive experiments on compositional out-of-distribution prompts demonstrate that both methods significantly outperform competitive baselines (DPO, KTO), while approaching oracle performance across multiple sampling temperatures, model scales and protein families. Moreover, PLMs fine-tuned with unsupervised rewards can achieve consistently higher coverage compared to their base model in pass@k evaluations. By enabling self-improvement of PLMs through their own generated experience, our framework provides a scalable pathway toward controllable biomolecular design in settings where labeled preferences or experimental feedback are scarce or unavailable.

</details>


### [100] [Online Reward-Punishment Learning from Fixed-Channel Perceptual Event Streams without Environment Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18963)
*Zirong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需外部标量奖励/评价标签的在线奖惩学习框架OHIRL，仅通过固定通道感知数据包和内部残差调节评估器推断奖惩效价，在多种任务上达到高准确率，并验证了其组件必要性和信息边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统强化学习依赖外部标量奖励或评价标签的局限，探索仅凭感知过渡结果（如疼痛、能量等）推断奖惩效价的自主学习范式。

Method: 将学习分解为四个角色：M_psi（下一数据包预测）、D_omega（残差动力学建模）、C_eta（固定内部轨迹后评估器，遵循恢复正性/持久负性调节取向）、B_xi（利用C_eta的价值证据进行策略更新与动作评分）。采用条件误差分解分离证据估计误差与策略优化误差。

Result: 在2x2-XOR任务中，药物与辣椒在视觉XOR上下文中获得相反效价；B_xi达到0.952平衡奖励符号准确率。完整在线审计中M_psi的留出R2=0.907，B_xi符号准确率0.940，策略最优动作准确率0.979；各类消融对照组（即时数据包评分、预测误差奖励等）性能崩溃。

Conclusion: OHIRL框架无需外部奖励信号即可从感知过渡中推断奖惩效价并指导策略学习，系数来源审计验证了其评估取向的稳健性（等单元、原始等值、随机单调变体保留>92%排名，符号反转保留0%），消融实验证明了各模块的必要性。

Abstract: We study online reward-punishment learning when the environment provides no scalar reward or evaluative label. At each step the agent receives only a fixed-channel perceptual packet, and quantities such as pain, energy, contact, damage, or cognitive error are treated as perceptual dimensions whose valence must be inferred from transition consequences. OHIRL separates four roles: M_psi learns next-packet prediction, D_omega models residual dynamics, C_eta is a fixed internal post-transition trajectory evaluator, and B_xi learns to use the resulting value evidence for later policy updates and action scoring. C_eta uses a recovery-positive and persistence/growth-negative residual-regulation orientation; a coefficient-origin audit shows that equal-unit, raw-equal, and random monotone variants preserve more than 92% of the released top-action rankings, while sign inversion preserves 0%.
  The reward-free protocol exposes observation transitions while withholding environment rewards, delayed external evaluators, success labels, and action-goodness labels. A conditional error decomposition separates B_xi evidence-estimation error from residual policy-optimization error. In a 2x2-XOR packet task, medicine and chili acquire opposite value under visual XOR contexts, and the same pain or spice increase can be positive or negative depending on consequence structure; B_xi reaches 0.952 balanced reward-sign accuracy. In a full online-interleaved audit, M_psi reaches holdout R2=0.907, B_xi reaches 0.940 sign accuracy, and the policy reaches 0.979 optimal-action accuracy, while immediate packet scores, prediction-error rewards, shuffled targets, zero reward, and error-reduction controls collapse. Hidden-reward CartPole and Taxi controls, public-context no-leakage audits, and module-role ablations further test information boundaries and component necessity.

</details>


### [101] [EfficientRollout: System-Aware Self-Speculative Decoding for RL Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18967)
*Minseo Kim,Minjae Lee,Seunghyuk Oh,Kevin Galim,Donghoon Kim,Coleman Hooper,Harman Singh,Amir Gholami,Hyung Il Koo,Wonjun Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EfficientRollout 是一个系统感知的自推测解码框架，通过量化目标模型生成草稿模型、设计系统感知的推测解码切换策略和接受感知的草稿长度自适应机制，在不损害模型质量的前提下，将强化学习 rollout 阶段及端到端延迟分别降低最多 19.6% 和 12.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为LLM主流后训练范式，但 rollout 生成中的自回归解码导致严重的延迟瓶颈；虽然推测解码（SD）理论上可降低延迟，但其实际加速效果无法直接迁移到 RL rollout 场景，原因在于：(i) 目标策略不断演化导致固定草稿模型与策略分布失配；(ii) 活跃批次大小在解码过程中持续缩小，使计算从计算受限转向内存受限，并行验证难以充分利用空闲算力。

Method: EfficientRollout 包含三个核心设计：(1) 对目标模型进行量化得到草稿模型（自推测解码），无需单独预训练或在线适应，使草稿模型始终与演化中的策略保持同步；(2) 系统感知的 SD 切换策略，只在有益的计算区间启用推测解码；(3) 接受感知的草稿长度自适应机制，根据草稿模型实时质量动态调整草稿生成预算。

Result: 在加速的自回归 rollout 基线基础上，EfficientRollout 将 rollout 延迟降低最多 19.6%，端到端延迟降低最多 12.7%，且不损害最终模型质量。

Conclusion: EfficientRollout 通过系统感知的自推测解码框架，有效解决了推测解码在 RL rollout 场景中的分布失配和计算模式转换问题，实现了显著的延迟加速。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a representative post-training paradigm for LLMs, enabling strong reasoning and agentic capabilities. However, rollout generation remains a dominant latency bottleneck because autoregressive sampling decodes responses sequentially and a small number of long-tailed generations often determine completion time. Speculative decoding (SD) offers a natural way to address this bottleneck, as it is a well-established technique for serving fixed LLMs that reduces latency by rapidly drafting tokens and accepting them through parallel verification while preserving the target-model distribution. However, its practical speedups do not directly carry over to RL rollouts: (i) the evolving target policy makes any fixed drafter increasingly mismatched with the policy's output distribution; and (ii) active batch sizes shrink throughout rollout decoding, shifting decoding from compute-bound to memory-bound regimes where parallel verification can exploit underutilized compute. Therefore, accelerating RL rollouts requires both a drafter that remains effective under long, high-temperature generations from an evolving policy and system-aware use of SD that avoids compute-bound regimes. We present EfficientRollout, a system-aware self-SD framework designed to address this gap for RL rollouts. EfficientRollout induces a quantized drafter from the target model (i.e. self-speculative decoding), keeping it coupled to the evolving policy without separate drafter pretraining or online adaptation. It further coordinates a system-aware SD toggle policy with acceptance-aware draft-length adaptation, enabling speculation only in beneficial regimes while matching the drafting budget to evolving drafter quality. EfficientRollout reduces rollout and end-to-end latency by up to 19.6% and 12.7%, respectively, over an accelerated AR rollout baseline, while preserving final model quality.

</details>


### [102] [DIPHINE: Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18997)
*Simon Pedro Galeano Munoz,Mustapha Bounoua,Giulio Franzese,Pietro Michiardi,Maurizio Filippone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIPHINE，首个基于扩散模型的神经估计器，可对连续非高斯动力系统计算集成信息分解（ΦID）的全部16个信息原子，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ΦID计算方法局限于高斯或离散系统，无法应用于连续非高斯动力系统，严重限制了该框架在真实复杂系统中的应用。

Method: 利用基于分数匹配的扩散模型，通过单个摊销网络联合估计ΦID所需的全部互信息项，再通过Möbius反演恢复16个信息原子，并提供了误差传播的理论分析。

Result: 在合成基准上准确恢复了真实信息原子；相比现有互信息估计器表现更优；在真实数据上无需分布假设即可提取可解释的信息动力学结构。

Conclusion: DIPHINE成功将ΦID的应用范围扩展至连续非高斯动力系统，为复杂系统的信息架构分析提供了通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Uncovering the true informational architecture of real-world complex systems requires disentangling how their components uniquely store, redundantly share, and synergistically integrate information over time. Integrated Information Decomposition ($Φ$ID) is a framework for decomposing the information dynamics of multivariate systems into sixteen non-overlapping atoms that characterize redundant, unique, and synergistic modes of information storage, transfer, and integration. Existing methods to compute $Φ$ID are restricted to Gaussian or discrete systems, preventing its application to continuous non-Gaussian dynamical systems. We address this limitation by proposing DIPHINE (Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator), the first neural estimator that leverages score-based diffusion models to jointly estimate all the mutual information terms required by $Φ$ID from a single amortized network, recovering the sixteen atoms through Möbius inversion. We provide a theoretical analysis of error propagation through the inversion, showing that the Jacobian of the mapping from mutual informations to atoms is integer-valued and that the synergy-to-synergy atom is provably the hardest to estimate. We demonstrate accurate recovery of ground-truth atoms on synthetic benchmarks, superior performance compared to established mutual information estimators, and the ability to extract physiologically interpretable information-dynamic structure on an application involving real data without any distributional assumptions.

</details>


### [103] [Geometric and Stochastic Analysis of Discontinuities in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19036)
*Tho Tran Huu,Huu-Tuan Nguyen,Thien-Hai Nguyen,Nhat-Tri Ho,Viet-Hoang Tran,Tho Quan,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对稀疏混合专家（SMoE）架构中的不连续性进行了严格的几何与随机分析，按阶次分类不连续面，证明低阶不连续占主导地位，并基于此提出一种轻量级平滑机制，在保持计算开销的同时提升模型连续性与实际性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMoE中Top-k专家选择机制导致映射函数在切换边界处不连续，即使输入无限接近也可能激活不同专家集合，产生显著差异输出。现有工作缺乏对此不连续性的严格理论分析，需要深入理解其几何结构与随机行为。

Method: 1) 按切换事件中并列专家数量对不连续面进行阶次分类；2) 利用测度论切片论证建立增厚不连续面的渐近体积估计；3) 将输入空间随机扰动建模为扩散过程，证明路径几乎必然遇到不连续面，且首次击中几乎总发生在阶-1不连续面，并给出有限时间概率界；4) 推导各阶不连续面邻域停留时间的界限；5) 提出平滑机制，在不连续面附近软性地纳入专家输出，保证计算开销小。

Result: 低阶不连续集占主导地位，高阶不连续集占据渐近可忽略的相对体积；随机路径几乎必然在有限时间内击中不连续面，且首次击中几乎总在阶-1不连续面；所提平滑机制在语言和视觉任务上不仅恢复了映射连续性，还提升了经验性能。

Conclusion: 输入更可能位于低阶不连续面附近；提出的平滑机制简单有效，可直接应用于现有SMoE，在极小计算开销下实现局部平滑，同时提升模型性能。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) architectures are now widely deployed in state-of-the-art language and vision models, where conditional routing allows scaling to very large networks. However, this very Top-$k$ expert selection that enables conditional routing also renders the SMoE map inherently discontinuous. In the vicinity of these discontinuity surfaces, even inputs that are arbitrarily close may activate substantially different sets of experts resulting in significantly different outputs. In this work we give a rigorous geometric and stochastic analysis of these discontinuities. We first classify them by order, determined by the number of tied experts at a switching event. Using measure-theoretic slicing arguments, we establish asymptotic volume estimates for the thickened discontinuity surfaces, showing that lower-order discontinuity sets dominate, whereas higher-order ones occupy a vanishingly small relative volume. Next, modeling random perturbations in the input space via a diffusion process, we prove that the path eventually encounter a discontinuity, and moreover that the first hit almost surely occurs on an order-1 discontinuity with explicit finite-time probability bounds. We further derive occupation-time bounds that quantify the duration the random path spend in the neighborhoods of each discontinuity order. These theoretical results imply that inputs are more likely to lie near lower order discontinuities. Motivated by this insight, we propose a simple smoothing mechanism that can be directly applied to existing SMoEs, softly incorporating experts near discontinuities; our analysis guarantees that the added computational overhead remains small while providing localized smoothing near discontinuities, and experiments across language and vision tasks show that smoothing not only enforces continuity of the SMoE map but also enhances empirical performance.

</details>


### [104] [Smoothness-Based Derandomization of PAC-Bayes Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19105)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑损失函数下PAC-Bayes去随机化方法，通过利用损失函数和预测器类的光滑性，获得确定性预测器的高概率泛化界。从Gibbs预测器到后验均值确定性预测器的转换代价由Jensen间隙类的泛化差距精确刻画，通过Rademacher复杂度控制该类，得到涉及参数Jacobian和Hessian平坦度量的确定性预测器界。该框架适用于有界/无界光滑损失函数，并特化到线性预测器和光滑神经网络。理论中的Jacobian和Hessian量启发了一种实用正则化器，在CIFAR-10上实验展示了其在不同批大小下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAC-Bayes方法通常给出随机化预测器（如Gibbs预测器）的泛化界，但实际部署中需要使用确定性预测器。本文旨在利用损失函数和预测器类的光滑性，建立确定性预测器与随机化预测器之间泛化性能的精确关系，从而获得确定性预测器的高概率泛化保证。

Method: 利用Jensen间隙类刻画从Gibbs预测器到后验均值预测器的转换代价，通过Rademacher复杂度控制该类；推导涉及参数Jacobian和Hessian的平坦度量化界；将理论结果特化到线性预测器和光滑神经网络；基于理论中的Jacobian和Hessian量设计实用正则化器，对BatchNorm网络通过折叠BN变换计算该正则化器。

Result: 建立了平滑损失下PAC-Bayes去随机化的精确代价公式，得到了确定性预测器的高概率泛化界，其中界依赖于参数的Jacobian和Hessian平坦度量。在CIFAR-10上展示了所提正则化器在不同批大小下的行为。

Conclusion: 本文成功将PAC-Bayes分析扩展到确定性预测器场景，通过光滑性假设建立了理论基础，并导出了实用的正则化方法。实验验证了理论量的实际效用。

Abstract: We study PAC-Bayes derandomization for smooth loss functions. Our goal is to obtain generalization bounds that hold with high probability for deterministic predictors by exploiting smoothness properties of both the loss and the predictor class. We show that passing from the Gibbs predictor to the deterministic predictor at the posterior mean has a precise cost, given by the generalization gap of the Jensen gap class. We control this class through its Rademacher complexity, leading to bounds for deterministic predictors that involve flatness quantities expressed in terms of parameter Jacobians and Hessians of the score map. The framework applies to both bounded and unbounded smooth loss functions, and we specialize the results to linear predictors and smooth neural networks. Finally, the Jacobian and Hessian quantities appearing in the theory motivate a practical regularizer. For BatchNorm networks, we compute this regularizer with respect to effective BatchNorm weights obtained by folding the BatchNorm transformation into the adjacent affine weights. Experiments on CIFAR-10 illustrate the behavior of this regularizer under different batch sizes.

</details>


### [105] [JourneyFormer: Encoding Airbnb Guest Journey with Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19108)
*Daochen Zha,Chun How Tan,Xin Liu,Bin Xu,Han Zhao,Xiaowei Liu,Tracy Yu,Hui Gao,Huiji Gao,Liwei He,Stephanie Moyerman,Sanjeev Katariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Airbnb部署的序列模型JourneyFormer，用于搜索排序，通过解决序列复杂度高和标签稀疏等生产挑战，在离线指标和在线A/B测试中均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列建模虽理论简单，但在实际生产中面临序列复杂（如Airbnb中客人序列长、探索性强）和标签稀疏（仅关注预订标签）等挑战，需在设计上平衡效果与可扩展性。

Method: 提出JourneyFormer，涵盖用户事件选择、ID嵌入、模型架构和标签归因等关键设计，并采用加速训练和推理的定制策略。

Result: 在Airbnb生产系统中成功部署，离线排序指标提升，并在2个产品表面的在线A/B测试中关键业务指标显著增长。

Conclusion: JourneyFormer有效解决了生产环境中的序列建模挑战，证明了其在搜索排序中的实用价值和业务影响力。

Abstract: Sequence modeling has become increasingly popular in recommendation and ranking algorithms, owing to its capacity to model users' historical behaviors and infer user intentions. Despite its theoretical simplicity, the practical deployment of a sequence model in production is non-trivial due to complexity of the sequence and sparse labels. For example, in Airbnb, guest sequences are often long, exploratory and complex, and we focus on booking labels, which are sparse. As such, we are often required to make various design decisions regarding data and modeling to strike a balance between effectiveness and scalability. This work delved into these production challenges and deployed JourneyFormer, a sequence modeling solution for search ranking at Airbnb. We detail crucial design considerations, covering aspects such as guest event selection, ID embeddings, model architecture, and label attribution. Additionally, we describe several tailored strategies to accelerate model training and inference. JourneyFormer has been successfully deployed within Airbnb's production, where its effectiveness and impact have been evidenced not only by improved offline ranking metrics but also by significant gains in key business metrics through online A/B testing across 2 production surfaces.

</details>


### [106] [Seeing Before Reasoning: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Shortcut-Resilient Multimodal On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19120)
*Sihan Wang,Xiyao Liu,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ViGOS框架，通过视觉描述+双教师蒸馏解决多模态大模型在自蒸馏中的捷径问题，提升图像基础行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）在LLM推理中表现良好，但直接扩展到多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）会产生捷径：特权目标可能主要基于文本参考目标而非图像指导token生成，导致模型忽视视觉信息。

Method: ViGOS框架：学生先写视觉描述再推理至最终答案。利用两个教师模型——仅图像感知的感知教师监督视觉描述，特权推理教师监督同一学生前缀上的推理和最终答案。仅对无效rollout使用参考教师恢复输出格式。

Result: 在通用视觉语言、专家推理、视觉数学、空间定位和视觉语言先验基准测试中，ViGOS保持了OPSD的主要优势，并在易产生捷径的设置中改善了图像基础行为。

Conclusion: ViGOS有效解决了MLLM中OPSD的视觉捷径问题，通过引入视觉描述和视觉感知教师，确保模型真正基于图像内容进行推理，而非仅依赖文本模式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a model on its own rollouts and uses a frozen copy to provide dense token-level targets conditioned on a reference target. This works well for LLM reasoning, but a direct extension to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can create a shortcut: the privileged target may guide tokens mainly based on the text reference target rather than the image. We propose ViGOS, a visually grounded OPSD framework for MLLM post-training. The student first writes a visual description and then reasons toward the final answer. For valid rollouts, an image-only perception teacher supervises the description, while a privileged reasoning teacher supervises the reasoning and final answer on the same student prefix. A reference teacher is used only for invalid rollouts to recover the output format. Across general vision-language, expert reasoning, visual math, spatial grounding, and visual-language-prior benchmarks, ViGOS keeps the main benefits of OPSD and improves image-grounded behavior in shortcut-prone settings.

</details>


### [107] [INDEQS: Informed Neural controlled Differential EQuationS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19138)
*Michael Detzel,Gabriel Nobis,Kristiyan Blagov,Juri Schubert,Jackie Ma,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INDEQS是一种将已知有向图先验知识融入图神经受控微分方程（NCDE）的时间序列预测方法，通过内外混合机制分离图节点间的隐状态混合与向量场-控制混合，在合成平流模拟和真实水文、交通预测任务上优于无先验的NCDE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图NCDE方法通常完全从数据学习空间结构，但许多实际场景中已知有向图结构（如水系网络、交通路网），现有方法未能有效利用这一先验知识。

Method: INDEQS将图先验知识分别注入架构的不同位置：内混合（inner mixing）负责图节点间隐状态传播，外混合（outer mixing）负责向量场与控制信号之间的交互；提供轻量级图约束变体和通过自适应图卷积学习额外连接的更具表达力的变体。

Result: 在合成平流模拟和两个真实任务（河流水位预测、PeMS08交通流预测）上，外信息注入（outer informedness）在参数相当的情况下一致优于无先验NCDE，尤其在更大图上优势明显；内信息注入（inner informedness）在严格遵循已知邻接矩阵时提供更参数高效的替代方案；连续时间解码器优于离散卷积解码器。

Conclusion: 将已知图结构先验注入NCDE架构可显著提升预测精度，外混合是先验知识最有效的注入位置，连续时间解码器在实际任务中具有更好的精度和时间灵活性。

Abstract: Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDE) provide a powerful continuous-time framework for forecasting time series, but standard graph-based extensions typically learn spatial structure purely from data, even in settings where a directed graph structure is known a priori. We introduce Informed Neural controlled Differential EQuationS (INDEQS), a graph-based NCDE forecasting method that incorporates prior knowledge of a directed graph at distinct architectural positions. INDEQS separates inner mixing of hidden states across graph nodes from outer mixing between vector field and control, and offers both a lightweight graph-constrained variant and a more expressive variant, learning additional graph connections from data via adaptive graph convolutions. To systematically study when graph informedness is beneficial in forecasting, we devise a continuous advection simulation on directed graphs, yielding synthetic spatio-temporal datasets with known ground-truth flow structure. We then evaluate INDEQS on two real-world tasks: river discharge forecasting on a hydrological network and traffic flow prediction on PeMS08. Across these synthetic and real-world benchmarks, outer informedness consistently improves mean absolute error over an uninformed NCDE with comparable parameter count, particularly on larger graphs, while inner informedness offers a more parameter-efficient alternative when strict adherence to a known adjacency is desired. A comparison of discrete convolutional and continuous-time decoders further shows that continuous decoders yield better accuracy and greater temporal flexibility on real-world tasks. An implementation of INDEQS and the advection simulation is available at https://github.com/Mitchi1/indeqs.

</details>


### [108] [ChronoSurv: A Clinical Pathway-Guided Graph Framework for Multimodal Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19140)
*Hugo Miccinilli,Theo Di Piazza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChronoSurv 提出了一种异构层次化有向图框架，通过构建患者诊疗轨迹的有向图结构，结合异构消息传递和层次化拓扑，实现对头颈癌多模态生存数据的精准预测，在两个公开数据集上达到最优判别性能与可靠校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生存模型依赖静态融合策略或时间无关建模，无法捕捉结构化临床工作流程中复杂的时序依赖与模态间非对称关系，亟需一种轨迹感知的建模方法。

Method: 构建进展感知的临床轨迹有向图，与关键诊断步骤对齐；设计层次化拓扑包含细粒度、粗粒度和全局表示；采用异构消息传递机制建模模态间及临床步骤间的复杂非对称关系；支持灵活适应缺失模态。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上取得最优判别性能，并保持统计可靠的校准效果；消融实验验证了各架构组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的图建模方法能够有效利用多模态临床数据的结构化时序信息，为多模态生存预测提供新范式。
